In fact, Jana and Suta are- the only

Kshattriyas are divided. The latter,
however, comprises within itself four
different sections viz., (1) Chagrami
or Chagnaya, (2) Paschima Taraffi, (3)
Kashipuri, and (4) Baragrami. These
sub-sections, as is evident, derived their
names from the locality they settled.in
and have very little difference from
the main body of the Suta

ita sub-caste
as regards the observance of social rites
and customs. But such is not the
case with the Jana sub-caste, The
difference existing between it and the
Suta sub-caste is comparatively greater
and intermarriage
